# Page packets — Prosperity

31 pages, 6.625 x 10.25 inches. Made by the writers' room; every packet below is complete on its own.

## How to use these packets

1. **Start with the sheets** below: the character sheet, then the location sheet. Paste each,
   keep the results, and attach them as reference images with every page that names them
   (each page packet says which). Approve one page as your style page and attach that too.
   For a long book, train a LoRA on the approved sheets and pages; references alone drift.
2. **One page at a time.** Paste everything under a page's heading, nothing else. Keep the
   same model and settings for the whole book so the style and the characters stay put.
3. **No text on the pages.** The words are added afterwards, in the room, as a layer over
   your art - so a beautiful model that cannot spell is fine. If the model draws any letters,
   regenerate; do not keep a page with text on it.
4. **Check the page** against its checklist before you keep it. One thing wrong: regenerate
   with the same packet, and only then adjust wording.
5. **Upload each kept page** in the room (Production, Lettering tab) and run the lettering:
   the room draws every balloon and caption where the layout put them, and you download the
   finished, lettered page.

## Rules for every page

- Never render text of any kind: no balloons, captions, sound effects, page numbers,
  titles, chapter names, signatures, watermarks, nameplates or labels.
- Keep the areas each panel lists as clear: uncluttered art (sky, wall, shadow) where a
  balloon will sit, never a face or the thing the panel is about.
- Draw every character exactly as described, every time. The description wins over the
  previous page's picture.
- Panel count, panel shape and reading order are fixed by the map. Do not add, merge or
  reshape panels.

## Character sheet — draw this first

_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one character reference sheet: every character below standing full length in a row, front view, neutral pose, even daylight, plain light background, in the book's style. Same scale for everyone so heights compare. No text, names, labels or captions anywhere on the sheet.

**Style:**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

He wears a patched charcoal work shirt, rust-brown utility

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 1 (right) — Older Alex chooses to watch while TJ comes apart over the lake.

**Panel 1.** NIGHT. At a lakeside boathouse, older Alex grips a mooring post as TJ steps from the dock and separates mid-stride. Alex’s granddaughter stands behind him in a blanket.

SFX: KLAK—KLAK—KLAK

**Panel 2.** Close on the girl and Alex. Across Lake Keel, a tanker barge and two siphon drones work beneath white lamps.

GRANDDAUGHTER: Is TJ broken?

ALEX: Not usually.

**Panel 3.** TJ’s head glides ahead; his torso and legs move independently toward the barge.

**Panel 4.** The girl catches Alex’s sleeve. He keeps watching.

GRANDDAUGHTER: Should we call someone?

ALEX: Let’s see what they came to take.

PAGE CHECK: Person, relationship and choice lead the opening.

---
```

## Page 2 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **THIEF 1** — (no description in the bible yet)
- **THIEF 2** — (no description in the bible yet)
- **GRANDDAUGHTER** — **Causality** - **Public role:** Repair-dog companion and access tool. - **Immediate want:** Reach places B11 and the humans cannot reach and return to them. - **Deeper need / contradiction:** He is assembled as a tool but behaves as a companion. - **Leverage / capability:** Separates into independently moving parts, scouts ducts and gaps, carries a camera and reaches mechanisms. - **Vulnerability:** Small size, fragile mismatched parts and the possibility of losing a component. - **Mistaken bel
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen years old, lean and wiry from pump-hall and mine work, approximately 5'9" tall, with narrow shoulders, strong forearms and scarred, work-rough hands. He has medium-brown skin, a broad Indigenous Andean-inherited nose and cheekbones, a firm jaw and dark brown eyes whose shape visibly echoes his European-descended father Adrian; his thick black hair is cut short with an uneven, self-trimmed edge. He wears a patched charcoal work shirt, rust-brown utility
- **TJ** — TJ is a small silent repair robot shaped unmistakably like a dog, approximately 14 inches high at the shoulder when assembled, built from mismatched magnetic ceramic, aluminum and painted steel parts. His head, four legs, torso, tail and ears can separate and work independently before snapping back together. In 2043 his two camera lenses are plain scratched ceramic, and his body carries rust, tool marks and mismatched paint. His silhouette is compact, dog-shaped and slightly lo

**The page's script, for reference:**

```text
## Page 2 (left) — TJ reaches the thieves while Alex lets himself be seen.

**Panel 1.** TJ’s head crosses the lake. His body and legs travel along a half-submerged maintenance rail. The tanker hose churns behind him.

SFX: SHHHRRRR

**Panel 2.** Two thieves monitor the hose. Beyond them, dry terraces descend toward the water.

THIEF 1: Keep the pull low.

THIEF 2: Clean water doesn’t stay unclaimed.

**Panel 3.** TJ’s head angles toward a drone intake.

**Panel 4.** From shore, Alex and the girl stand in the sweep of the lamps.

GRANDDAUGHTER: They can see us.

ALEX: Good.

PAGE CHECK: Scarcity is visible; Alex deliberately refuses concealment.

---
```

```text
## Page 3 (right) — The thieves reduce Alex’s prosperity to one accusation.

**Panel 1.** One thief studies the boathouse through binoculars.

THIEF 1: That’s Phantum’s place.

**Panel 2.** The tanker gauge climbs.

THIEF 1: How’d he get his money?

THIEF 2: Water.

**Panel 3.** The thieves glance toward the dark boathouse.

THIEF 1: He owns the lake.

**Panel 4.** TJ’s head reaches the drone. His body and legs close in below.

PAGE CHECK: The question is now how Alex got what he has.

---
```

## Page 5 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

- **TJ** — TJ is a small silent repair robot shaped unmistakably like a dog, approximately 14 inches high at the shoulder when assembled, built from mismatched magnetic ceramic, aluminum and painted steel parts. His head, four legs, torso, tail and ears can separate and work independently before snapping back together. In 2043 his two camera lenses are plain scratched ceramic, and his body carries rust, tool marks and mismatched paint. His silhouette is compact, dog-shaped and slightly lo

- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **TJ** — TJ is a small silent repair robot shaped unmistakably like a dog, approximately 14 inches high at the shoulder when assembled, built from mismatched magnetic ceramic, aluminum and painted steel parts. His head, four legs, torso, tail and ears can separate and work independently before snapping back together. In 2043 his two camera lenses are plain scratched ceramic, and his body carries rust, tool marks and mismatched paint. His silhouette is compact, dog-shaped and slightly lo

## Page 46 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **LINA** — Dr. Lina Vey is a woman in her late fifties, approximately 5'5" tall, stooped slightly from years at fabrication benches, with medium-brown Indigenous Andean/mestiza skin, short black-and-silver hair, deep-set brown eyes and a long, serious face. She wears a stained slate maintenance uniform beneath a technical coat, reinforced shoes and a compact data-shard case clipped at her hip. Her silhouette is narrow, bent toward the work and immediately recognizable by the way she keeps
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is twenty years old, tall and steady at approximately 5'8", with a strong, practical build shaped by Keel walking and carrying work. She has warm brown skin, pronounced cheekbones, a broad expressive mouth, freckles across her nose and cheeks, light teal-blue eyes and long copper-brown hair that is usually wind-tangled and tied low with loose strands around her face; her features visibly resemble her Indigenous Andean/mestiza mother Leona while retaining lighter color
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen years old, lean and wiry from pump-hall and mine work, approximately 5'9" tall, with narrow shoulders, strong forearms and scarred, work-rough hands. He has medium-brown skin, a broad Indigenous Andean-inherited nose and cheekbones, a firm jaw and dark brown eyes whose shape visibly echoes his European-descended father Adrian; his thick black hair is cut short with an uneven, self-trimmed edge. He wears a patched charcoal work shirt, rust-brown utility

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is twenty years old, tall and steady at approximately 5'8", with a strong, practical build shaped by Keel walking and carrying work. She has warm brown skin, pronounced cheekbones, a broad expressive mouth, freckles across her nose and cheeks, light teal-blue eyes and long copper-brown hair that is usually wind-tangled and tied low with loose strands around her face; her features visibly resemble her Indigenous Andean/mestiza mother Leona while retaining lighter color
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen years old, lean and wiry from pump-hall and mine work, approximately 5'9" tall, with narrow shoulders, strong forearms and scarred, work-rough hands. He has medium-brown skin, a broad Indigenous Andean-inherited nose and cheekbones, a firm jaw and dark brown eyes whose shape visibly echoes his European-descended father Adrian; his thick black hair is cut short with an uneven, self-trimmed edge. He wears a patched charcoal work shirt, rust-brown utility

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

## Page 54 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADRIAN** — Adrian Phantum is a European-descended man in his forties in the surviving recordings, approximately 5'11" tall with a lean engineer's build, medium-light olive skin, dark brown hair receding slightly at the temples, a strong jaw and dark eyes whose shape and habitual head tilt visibly echo in Alex. He wears worn technical shirts, a patched work jacket, practical trousers and a stained engineer's apron in the workshop; in gambling memories he wears the same clothes with casino
- **B11** — B11 is a small foldable archival robot approximately 18 inches tall when upright, with a compact rectangular torso, two short articulated arms, two broad folding legs, a rounded shoulder projector and a chest compartment. His casing is matte cream and worn graphite with scratches, dust in the seams and small exposed brass fasteners. He has two warm amber optical eyes, including a brass ring around his right eye that clicks or rotates when he focuses, and he wears an old faded r

## Page 56 of 64 — Prosperity

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

- **MERA** — Mera Vale is a woman in her late fifties, tall and slender at approximately 5'10", with pale olive skin, silver-templed black hair cut in a precise jaw-length shape and dark brown eyes that remain controlled even under pressure. She wears pearl, graphite and cool-blue formal clothing with clean geometric seams, a graphite cuff and no visible House crest. Her silhouette is vertical, symmetrical and administratively severe. ## Page 59 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen years old, lean and wiry from pump-hall and mine work, approximately 5'9" tall, with narrow shoulders, strong forearms and scarred, work-rough hands. He has medium-brown skin, a broad Indigenous Andean-inherited nose and cheekbones, a firm jaw and dark brown eyes whose shape visibly echoes his European-descended father Adrian; his thick black hair is cut short with an uneven, self-trimmed edge. He wears a patched charcoal work shirt, rust-brown utility
- **MERA** — Mera Vale is a woman in her late fifties, tall and slender at approximately 5'10", with pale olive skin, silver-templed black hair cut in a precise jaw-length shape and dark brown eyes that remain controlled even under pressure. She wears pearl, graphite and cool-blue formal clothing with clean geometric seams, a graphite cuff and no visible House crest. Her silhouette is vertical, symmetrical and administratively severe. Her signature detail is a glass of water that she habitu
- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is twenty years old, tall and steady at approximately 5'8", with a strong, practical build shaped by Keel walking and carrying work. She has warm brown skin, pronounced cheekbones, a broad expressive mouth, freckles across her nose and cheeks, light teal-blue eyes and long copper-brown hair that is usually wind-tangled and tied low with loose strands around her face; her features visibly resemble her Indigenous Andean/mestiza mother Leona while retaining lighter color

## Page 61 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the right-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **TOMAS** — Tomas is an older Indigenous Andean miner in his early sixties, approximately 5'7" tall with a broad, weathered build, dark brown skin, heavy forearms and a grey-black mustache. His face has deep eye lines, a broad nose and a permanently squinting expression from dust and work lights. He wears a patched charcoal mining jacket, reinforced trousers, battered boots, a scuffed helmet and a worn leather wrist guard marked with the balanced water-drop symbol. His silhouette is square

## Page 64 of 64 — Prosperity
_Copy everything under this heading and paste it into the image model._

Draw one finished comic book page: a portrait page, 6.625 x 10.25 inches (about 2:3), the left-hand page of the book, with 4 panels. Fully inked and colored, clean panel borders with even white gutters. NO TEXT anywhere on the page.

**Style (the same on every page):**

Inked graphic-novel realism with heavy contour, textured hatching, painterly digital color, expressive acting and tactile architectural detail. Systems appear through pressure, wear, queues, repairs, records, surveillance and consequence rather than explanatory interfaces.

Prosperity is one mixed high-desert Andean basin, not three ethnic enclaves. Indigenous Andean, Latin American, European-descended, Afro-descendant, diasporic, mixed and international populations live across all three cities. Class, access, occupation, health, clothing, grooming, speech, environment and institutional power create the principal visual distinctions.

- **Keel:** dense, wind-scoured and compressed; rust, charcoal, oxidized orange, dust, patched machinery, narrow channels and crowded housing.
- **Halyard:** productive and commercially polished; fabrication floors, laboratories, maker districts, engineered greenery, sharper geometry and House checkpoints.
- **Oasis:** spacious, quiet, pale and controlled; reservoirs, geometric gardens, formal transit, civic interiors and hidden service infrastructure.

Alpha has no face, body or consciousness. It appears through white-and-amber nodes, cold restriction displays, gates, records, data lines, drones and administrative typography.

B11 is small, foldable, visibly repaired, scarfed and expressive through two warm optical eyes. He is patient, plainspoken, questioning, dryly humorous and fallible; never a lecturer, butler, threat or human replacement. TJ is silent, dog-shaped and assembled from mismatched magnetic parts.

**Characters — draw them exactly as described:**

- **ADA** — Ada Veyra is twenty years old, tall and steady at approximately 5'8", with a strong, practical build shaped by Keel walking and carrying work. She has warm brown skin, pronounced cheekbones, a broad expressive mouth, freckles across her nose and cheeks, light teal-blue eyes and long copper-brown hair that is usually wind-tangled and tied low with loose strands around her face; her features visibly resemble her Indigenous Andean/mestiza mother Leona while retaining lighter color
- **ALEX** — Alex Phantum is seventeen years old, lean and wiry from pump-hall and mine work, approximately 5'9" tall, with narrow shoulders, strong forearms and scarred, work-rough hands. He has medium-brown skin, a broad Indigenous Andean-inherited nose and cheekbones, a firm jaw and dark brown eyes whose shape visibly echoes his European-descended father Adrian; his thick black hair is cut short with an uneven, self-trimmed edge. He wears a patched charcoal work shirt, rust-brown utility
- **TJ** — TJ is a small silent repair robot shaped unmistakably like a dog, approximately 14 inches high at the shoulder when assembled, built from mismatched magnetic ceramic, aluminum and painted steel parts. His head, four legs, torso, tail and ears can separate and work independently before snapping back together. In 2043 his two camera lenses are plain scratched ceramic, and his body carries rust, tool marks and mismatched paint. His silhouette is compact, dog-shaped and slightly lo

```text
## Page 64 (left) — Alex and Ada face futures neither the system nor each other can choose.

**Panel 1.** DAWN at the Keel overlook. The raised transit gate frames the route toward Halyard and Oasis.

**Panel 2.** Alex holds a completed travel application with a public queue receipt:

`APPLICATION RECEIVED — REVIEW QUEUE 18`

**Panel 3.** Ada holds her own application beside a signed Morrow & Sons work-share agreement. She folds neither.

ADA: You could go.

**Panel 4.** TJ stands between them. Alex looks toward the gate. Ada looks toward the shop, then opens both papers flat in her hands.

ALEX: And you?

PAGE CHECK: Alex may leave or stay; Ada’s choice is equally concrete and unresolved.
```
